Theorem 219.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in hyperbolic theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 273 and the invariant I(A,B) = 316 mod 5.

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the hyperbolic decomposition. By minimality, the element a^273 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 316-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
